								local function fn72(arg, arg2)
									-- neat 2-column grid on the right side

										["TP Bat"] = { col = 2, row = 1 },

										["TP Down"] = { col = 1, row = 3 },
										["Carry Speed"] = { col = 2, row = 3 },
										["Bat Aimbot"] = { col = 1, row = 4 },
										["Auto Right"] = { col = 2, row = 4 },
										["Drop Brainrot"] = { col = 1, row = 5 },
										["Auto Left"] = { col = 2, row = 5 },
									})[arg2]

									local spacing = math.max(58, math.floor(52 * (_G._AdaptMobileBtnScale or 1) + 0.5) + 10)
									local rightPad = 14
									local topY = -150

									if v92 then
										-- col 1 = further left, col 2 = closer to edge
										local xOff = -(3 - v92.col) * spacing - rightPad
										local yOff = topY + (v92.row - 1) * spacing
										return UDim2.new(1, xOff, 0.5, yOff)
									end

									local c = (arg - 1) % 2
									local r = math.floor((arg - 1) / 2)
									return UDim2.new(1, -(2 - c) * spacing - rightPad, 0.5, topY + r * spacing)
								end
